
> Görev: Mevcut özellikleri Insider One tarzı bir arayüze taşı

  frontend/ altındaki Vue uygulamasında var olan TÜM özellikleri, Insider One
  InOne panelinin
  ekran yapısına ve görsel diline uyarla.
  - Sadece Vue: başka framework ya da düz HTML yok. Mevcut Vue kurulumu ve
  kütüphaneleri kullanılacak.
  - Hiçbir mevcut özellik kaybolmayacak. Benim onayım olmadan yeni özellik
  eklenmeyecek.
  - API çağrıları, store'lar ve iş mantığı korunacak, sadece ekran yapısı ve
  görünüm değişecek.
  - Insider logosu, adı ve görselleri kullanılmayacak.

  Kural: Ben "ONAYLIYORUM" yazana kadar mevcut dosyaları değiştirme.

  kendi

› Message from general-purpose
  ## Insider One AI agent and editor UI research

  **Method.** I read `llms.txt` (a flat index of `.md` pages; the agent pages
  are not in the first 100k characters). Then I read the 4 doc pages and the
  marketing page. WebFetch saved 9 of the screenshots locally and I looked at
  them as images: 5 from email-campaign-agent, 3 from sms-campaign-agent, 1 from
  new-email-drag-and-drop-editor. No screenshot has alt text or a caption.
  generate-an-email-template-with-ai has no images. The marketing page gave only
  text and image filenames, no CSS. I created or modified no files; WebFetch
  cached the images under
  `C:\Users\USER\.claude\projects\c--insider-one-case\...\tool-results\`.

  Source keys:
  - **[EA]** = /v1/docs/email-campaign-agent
  - **[SA]** = /docs/sms-campaign-agent
  - **[GT]** = /docs/generate-an-email-template-with-ai
  - **[DD]** = /v1/docs/new-email-drag-and-drop-editor
  - **[MK]** = insiderone.com/email-marketing-software

  ### 1. AI agent UX
  **Seen:**
  - **Where it lives:** a floating chat panel over the normal list page [EA
  img1]. It sits bottom-left, about half the screen wide.
    - Header: sidebar toggle, title ("New Chat" or the chat name), split-view
  icon, expand/collapse icon, X.
  - **Empty state:** small text "Welcome back.", a large heading "How can I help
  you today?", and a 2x2 grid of suggestion cards [EA img1]. The cards are
  white with a light border and about 6px radius, e.g. "Show me CTR rates for
  the last 30 days".
  - **Prompt box:** pinned to the bottom of the panel, multi-line, white,
  rounded, soft shadow, placeholder "Ask anything to get insights".
    - Bottom row: a "/" slash-command icon on the left and a send arrow on the
  right [EA].
    - Richer variant [SA img2]: "+" attach button, "/" icon, a removable
  lavender agent chip "Insider Agent x", a mic icon and a send button.
  - **It is a chat, not a form.**
    - User messages are right-aligned grey bubbles with a "You - 20:42"
  timestamp underneath.
    - Agent replies are plain text with no bubble [EA img3, SA img1].
  - **Progress while working [EA img3]:**
    - A sparkle "AI" icon, the line "Working on 2 tasks" and a "Campaign Agent
  ▾" expander.
    - The send button turns into a square stop button.
    - The prompt box border becomes a pink-to-purple gradient.
    - "Show Details ▾" collapses the agent's steps [EA img2/4, SA img1].
  - **Generated output:**
    - A blueprint card appears in the chat: icon tile, title "Autumn Collection
  Announcement Email (v4)", date, and a pink-to-purple gradient border.
    - Under it come a status sentence and a two-column summary table
  (ALAN/DETAY: Campaign, Channel, Status "Draft — not saved yet", Audience).
    - Clicking the card opens a right-hand split pane [EA img2/4, SA img1].
  - **Right pane:**
    - Title "Email Campaign" with a version badge "v4 ▾" that opens a version
  dropdown ("Version 6 / 02.06.2026 ...") [SA img2].
    - Tabs "Details | Preview".
    - Details is grouped into "Campaign Details / Segment Details / Content
  Details", each a label:value list with dividers.
    - Preview has a Desktop/Mobile segmented toggle (the active one is light
  blue) and the rendered email [EA img2].
    - Footer actions: "Create as New {Type}" or "Apply to ▾", plus a ⋮ menu [SA
  img2].
  - **Clarifying questions:** an inline question card, "Who should receive this
  SMS campaign?", with (i) and X icons [SA img1].
    - Option rows: "All reachable SMS subscribers" and "A specific audience /
  segment".
    - A free "Enter your answer..." field, a "1/1" pager, and "Submit ➤".
  - **Library:** a Blueprints page inside the AI panel, with a left nav (New
  Chat, Blueprints, Tasks, Settings, Search chats, ALL CHATS list) [SA img3].
    - Grouped grids (IMAGES, ARCHITECT, EMAIL, EUREKA), each with "See All" and
  filter dropdowns.

  **From the text:**
  - The email agent waits until it has audience, offer and CTA URL. It asks one
  question when audiences are ambiguous, and warns when the audience is 0 users
  and waits for "continue" [EA].
  - There is no explicit approve button for email: a complete brief saves the
  Draft automatically. No schedule is set, and launch is manual in the panel's
  **Launch** step [EA].
  - SMS has an explicit "Review and approve" summary step [SA].
    - The SMS agent validates length, segment count and personalization fields
  in the chat before saving.
    - A test SMS needs explicit approval.
  - Changes are requested in chat and each produces a new version/preview [EA,
  GT]. Figma import shows a preview in the chat that changes nothing until
  **Apply to Editor** [GT].

  ### 2. Editor layouts
  **Seen [DD img]:**
  - **Top bar:**
    - Left: "← Back to Design".
    - Centre: undo, redo, code `<>`, eye (preview), and a Desktop/Mobile
  segmented toggle (Desktop active, light-blue fill, "New" tag on Mobile).
    - Right: history, export, a brush (styles) icon, and a solid blue **Save**.
  - **Left:** a narrow vertical rail of square icon tiles. A top tile for
  structures, then image, text (T), button, spacer, social, divider, HTML, menu,
  video, timer.
  - **Canvas:** the email sits centred at 600px on a light grey #f6f6f6
  background.
  - **Right panel "Global Styles & Layout":** stacked rows of label + (?) help
  icon + control. Rows are separated by thin dividers.
    - Color field with hex text "#f6f6f6".
    - Stepper "− 600 +".
    - 3-way icon segmented control for alignment.
    - Blue toggles for Underline Links, Responsive Design, RTL.
    - Helper text under Responsive Design.

  **From the text [DD]:** real-time email size indicator, HTML/AMP preview with
  AMP errors highlighted, version history (named/tagged, restore from the ⋮
  menu), AI image generation ("generate with AI" link in the canvas copy), AI
  create/translate in 100+ languages that keeps merge tags intact, and shortcuts
  (L / Shift+L).

  **Not documented:** language or variant tabs.

  **SMS content editor:** none of the 5 pages shows or describes one. No
  character counter, segment count display, variable picker or phone preview was
  seen. [SA] only says the agent checks length and segments and uses real
  catalog personalization fields. Any UI for that would be inferred.

  ### 3. Visual language (seen in screenshots; hex values are my estimates)
  - **Primary:** royal/indigo blue, about #2B3BD6–#3045E0.
    - Solid Save button; tab underline and active tab text; toggles; links.
    - The "+ CREATE" button has a blue-to-violet gradient (about #2A3BD8 →
  #5B2FD0), uppercase label.
  - **AI accent:** a soft pastel mesh gradient across the chat panel background,
  pink about #F2C4D8, lavender about #C9C3EE, periwinkle about #B4BCE8.
    - Pink-to-purple gradient borders on blueprint cards and the active prompt
  box.
    - Magenta/purple sparkle icon.
    - Lavender chip with purple text (about #EDE7FB / #6B3FD1).
  - **Neutrals:** very dark navy icon rail (about #0B1220) with an active item
  in solid blue; page background about #F3F4F7; white surfaces; borders about
  #E3E5EA; grey user bubbles about #E4E6EC; table header labels in uppercase,
  small, letter-spaced grey.
  - **Type:** a geometric/neo-grotesk sans, Inter-like (inferred; never named).
    - Weights are mostly 400/500 with a 600 title. Large welcome heading about
  24px, body about 14px.
  - **Shapes and density:** radius about 4–8px on inputs, cards and buttons.
  Panels have about 8px radius and a soft wide shadow. Density is medium, with
  roomy rows in the settings panel.
  - **Icons:** thin outline line icons; stroke icons in the editor rail.

  ### 4. Reusable components seen
  - Slide-over / floating chat panel with split view
  - Prompt composer (slash, attach, mic, agent chip, send/stop)
  - Suggestion cards
  - Chat bubbles with timestamp
  - "Working on N tasks" collapsible status
  - Blueprint/result card with gradient border
  - Summary key-value table
  - Inline question card with options, pager and Submit
  - Version badge dropdown
  - Underline tabs (Details/Preview)
  - Desktop/Mobile segmented toggle
  - Right settings panel rows with (?) tooltips
  - Toggles, steppers, icon segmented control
  - Dropdown filters, search input, status pills ("Draft" grey tag)
  - Grid library with "See All"
  - Footer action bar ("Create as New" / "Apply to ▾" / ⋮)

  No toasts or modals were seen.

› Message from general-purpose
  Insider One Academy: email and SMS analytics screens

  **Method.** The llms.txt index lists pages as
  `https://academy.insiderone.com/docs/{slug}.md`. Its visible part has no email
  or SMS analytics entries; the rest of the index was cut off. I read all 5
  pages. Screenshots are on cdn.document360.io and none have alt text. I could
  view 11 of them directly (WebFetch saved the PNGs to its tool-results cache
  and I opened them; I created no files). Where I say "Seen" I looked at a
  screenshot. "Text" means the page's text. "Inferred" means my own judgment.

  Source keys: OA = v1/docs/overall-analytics, EA = email-analytics-page, ECA =
  email-campaign-analytics, OS = overall-sms-analytics, SS =
  single-sms-campaign-analytics.

  ### 1. Layout
  - **Page header (Seen: OA, EA, ECA).**
    - Breadcrumb at the top: home icon > Email > Analytics.
    - Large page title below it, light/regular weight, about 32px.
    - On the Email page, tabs under the title: Campaigns | **Analytics** |
  Templates. The active tab is blue text with a blue underline. The tab row sits
  on a thin divider line.
    - Under that, a "Date Range" label sits above a white input showing
  `24.05.2025 - 24.06.2025` with a calendar icon. Dates are DD.MM.YYYY.
    - Right-aligned actions:
      - EA: outlined blue "Filter" and "Reports ▾" buttons.
      - OA: a lavender "AI Summarize" button, plus grey filled "Export ▾" and
  "Actions ▾".
      - OS (Text): Filters and Export in the top right. Export sends an Excel
  file by email.
  - **Filters (Text).**
    - EA: Status, Type (single / experiment / recurring), Send Date, Tags.
    - OS: Message Type, Campaign Type, Status, Tags.
  - **KPI row (Seen: EA, ECA, OS).**
    - One wide white card split into 4 columns by thin vertical dividers, plus a
  separate narrower card for Revenue.
    - Each column has an uppercase, letter-spaced grey label with a (?) tooltip
  icon, then a large value (about 36px).
    - Under the value are 2 sub-rows: "label (?) ........ value", joined by a
  dotted leader line.
    - Email columns: SENT (Delivered, Delivery Rate) · OPEN RATE (Opens, Unique
  Opens) · CLICK-THROUGH RATE (Clicks, Unique Clicks) · CLICK-TO-OPEN RATE
  (Conversions, Conversion Rate) · REVENUE "62.4M USD".
    - SMS columns: TARGETED (Sent, Dropped) · DELIVERY RATE (Delivered,
  Undelivered) · CTR (Clicks) · CONVERSION RATE (Conversions) · REVENUE.
    - **No deltas or trend arrows appear** (Seen, and the text agrees).
  - **Charts.**
    - "PERFORMANCE BY CAMPAIGN METRICS" (Seen: ECA): a metric dropdown on the
  right, then a Day/Week/Month segmented control with the active segment light
  blue. Below it, a line chart in dark blue with a light blue area fill and
  dashed grid lines.
    - Subscriber Insights (Seen: EA): two side-by-side area charts. Subscribers
  is blue; Unsubscribers is amber/gold with a gradient fill. Each has 2 KPIs
  above it and an outlined "All Contacts" button.
    - Open Rate Breakdown: a column chart comparing real opens with MPP opens
  (Text: EA).
    - Top Devices and Top Email Clients: click distribution in % (Text: EA).
    - OA has a "Revenue by Channels" bar chart on an exponential scale (Text).
  - **Tables (Seen: EA Campaign Metrics).**
    - Header: "CAMPAIGN METRICS" with a "2 Campaigns" subtitle, a search input,
  and a grey "Metrics (16) ▾" column picker.
    - Column headers are uppercase on a light grey header row, with sort arrows
  and (?) icons.
    - The first column (Name) is sticky. The table scrolls horizontally and has
  a row kebab menu (blue).
    - Status shows as a green pill ("Completed").
    - Pagination: «  ‹  [1]  ›  » with the active page in a lavender square,
  "Showing 1-2 of 2 items", and an "items per page" input.
    - Full EA column list (Text): Campaign ID, Status, Type, Sent, Delivered,
  Delivery Rate, Open Rate, Opens, Unique Opens, CTR, Clicks, Unique Clicks,
  CTOR, Conversions, Conversion Rate, Revenue, Bounce Rate, Bounces, Blocks,
  Spam Reports, Unsubscribes.
    - OS tables (Text): Message Type Metrics (rows RCS Text / RCS Media / MMS /
  SMS / SMS Fallback) and Campaign Metrics (ID, Campaign Type, Status, Tags,
  Delivered, Clicks, Revenue, with more columns available through Metrics).
  - **Tabs.** No page-level Overview/Delivery/Engagement tabs. The only tabs are
  inside the SMS "Unsuccessful Messages" card: "Dropped (125,572) | Undelivered
  (95,474)" (Seen: OS/SS).
  - **Funnels.** None on any page (Text plus Seen).

  ### 2. Metrics
  - **Email (Text: EA, ECA).**
    - Sent = handed to the email provider after internal drops.
    - Delivered. Delivery Rate = Delivered / Sent.
    - Opens, Unique Opens. Open Rate = unique opens / delivered. It includes
  Apple MPP opens and is flagged as inflated.
    - Clicks, Unique Clicks. CTR = unique clicks / delivered. CTOR = unique
  clicks / unique opens.
    - Conversions use a 7-day window after a click. Conversion Rate = conv /
  delivered.
    - Revenue = Recommendation + Mobile App + Web revenue. Overall pages use
  last-click attribution; single campaigns use click-through attribution.
    - Bounce Rate = bounces / sent. Bounces = hard bounces. Blocks = soft,
  server-side rejections.
    - Spam Reports, Unsubscribers. Subscribers and their rates.
    - Seen: a separate 3-column strip with BOUNCE RATE, BLOCKS and SPAM REPORTS,
  using filled dark (i) icons.
  - **SMS (Text: OS, SS).**
    - Targeted, Sent, Dropped (never handed to the provider).
    - Delivered, Undelivered. Delivery Rate = delivered / sent.
    - Clicks. CTR = clicks / delivered.
    - Conversions (last-click within the window). Conversion Rate = conversions
  / delivered. Revenue.
    - Opens are RCS only.
    - Single SMS adds Message Parts, Unsubscriber Rate and Unsubscribers.
    - The term "Failed" is not used anywhere.
  - **Failure breakdown (Seen: EA/ECA "Dropped Messages", OS/SS "Unsuccessful
  Messages").**
    - A **semi-circle (half-donut) gauge chart**. Under it, a legend list:
  colored dot, reason name, (?) icon, dotted leader line, then a right-aligned
  "37.74% (40,000)".
    - Sorted from largest to smallest. Zero-value reasons show as grey "0.00%
  (0)". With no drops at all, the chart is a grey ring (Text).
    - Email reasons: Channel Frequency Capped, Content Failure, Default
  Frequency Capped, Duplicates, Sending Drops, Service Provider Errors, Coupon
  Lists, Product Recommendations, Localization, Invalid Dynamic Sender Address.
    - SMS dropped reasons: Invalid Phone Numbers, Frequency Capped, Coupon List
  Drops, Country Code Drops, Duplicates, Silent Hours, Internal Errors (plus MMS
  Drops per the OS text).
    - SMS undelivered reasons: Carrier Violation, Delivery Report Missing, Soft
  Bounces, Hard Bounces, Delivery Failures.

  ### 3. Single campaign view
  **Seen (ECA), from top to bottom:**
  1. Breadcrumb "Email > Analytics", the campaign name as the H1, and outlined
  "Recipients Activity" and "Export" buttons on the right.
  2. A meta strip card with a green "Completed" status pill, the campaign name,
  and blue "Edit" (pencil icon) and "Details" (info icon) links on the right.
  3. The KPI row plus the Revenue card.
  4. The Performance chart.

  **Text (ECA):** after the chart come Email Related Details (bounce, blocks,
  spam, unsubscribes, MPP %), then Dropped Messages, then Link Click Activity
  (URL, Clicks, Unique Clicks; shown per variant for experiments). Details opens
  schedule, segments, variations and design. Dates are not shown in the header.

  **Single SMS (Text: SS):** Essential KPIs, then Supporting KPIs, then
  Unsuccessful Messages, then Performance chart. A Details button sits at top
  right.

  **Seen (SS):** a two-column grid.
  - Top row: a Message Parts card on the left; Unsubscriber Rate and
  Unsubscribers on the right.
  - Next row: Unsuccessful Messages on the left; a LINK CLICK ACTIVITY table on
  the right (LINK URL, TOTAL CLICKS, paginated).

  ### 4. Visual language (Seen; hex values are my estimates)
  - **Backgrounds and surfaces.** Page background is cool light grey, about
  #F1F2F5. Cards are white with a hairline border about #E3E5EA and a very soft
  shadow (barely visible). Corner radius is small, about 4–6px. Dividers are
  about 1px #E5E7EB.
  - **Brand and accent colors.**
    - Primary is royal/cobalt blue, about #1F2FD1–#2233CC. It is used for active
  tabs, outlined buttons, links and the main chart line.
    - Secondary buttons are grey filled, about #E4E6EB.
    - The AI button is lavender, about #E6D9FF, with purple text.
    - On the OA product card, the Email card has an orange top border plus an
  orange icon tile, about #F0883E. Inferred: each channel has its own accent
  color.
  - **Status pill.** Light green background, about #E6F6EC, with green text,
  about #2E8B57.
  - **Chart palette (half-donuts).** Cobalt #1530C8, ochre #C97A2B, sky blue
  #4A90D9, deep purple #4A1F80, green #3D8A4A, mustard #E0AC45, red #D93030,
  crimson #C2255C, and grey for zero values. Unsubscriber charts use amber,
  about #E0B040.
  - **Typography.** A geometric sans, likely Inter or a similar face (Inferred;
  no font is named anywhere).
    - Labels are 11–12px, uppercase, letter-spaced about 0.1em, medium weight,
  grey-blue about #5A6478.
    - Values are 28–36px regular weight, near-black about #2A2F3A.
    - Sub-rows are 13–14px.
    - Section titles are uppercase, letter-spaced, grey (e.g., "PERFORMANCE BY
  CAMPAIGN METRICS").
  - **Density.** Airy: about 24–32px card padding and about 16–24px gaps between
  cards.
  - **Grid.** The KPI row is about 80/20 (wide card plus Revenue card). Detail
  sections use 2 columns, roughly 35/65 or 50/50.

  ### 5. Reusable components (Seen unless noted)
  - **KPI group card:** label with (?) icon, big value, and dotted-leader
  sub-metrics. Plus a simple single-stat card (bounce strip, Message Parts).
  - **Section card header:** uppercase title, one-line grey description with a
  "visit Academy" link, and actions on the right.
  - **Date-range input** with a calendar icon.
  - **Buttons:** outlined blue, grey filled with caret, and a "Metrics (N) ▾"
  picker that adds or removes metrics, max 6 on the OA cards.
  - **Segmented control:** Day/Week/Month.
  - **Charts:** line/area chart with gradient fill and dashed grid; half-donut
  with a dotted-leader legend; bar and column charts (Text).
  - **Underline tabs** with counts in the label.
  - **Data table:** sticky first column, sortable headers, status pill, row
  kebab menu, horizontal scroll, pagination with items-per-page.
  - **(?) info-icon tooltips** on every metric. Hovering a drop reason shows its
  definition (Text: EA).
  - **Empty and zero states:** a grey ring and grey 0.00% (0) rows; a notice
  when the selected range predates drop tracking (Text: EA).
  - **OA product summary card:** colored top border, channel icon tile, 6 KPI
  columns, a "View More Email Metrics" link in the footer, and a drag handle
  (⋮⋮).

› Message from general-purpose
  InOne panel UI research (read-only; I created and modified no files). I could
  view the screenshots: WebFetch saved each fetched PNG to the session's
  tool-results folder on its own, and I opened 11 of them with Read. Labels: [S]
  = seen in a screenshot, [T] = stated in page text, [I] = my inference.

  Source pages: EC = email-campaigns-page, AN = anatomy-of-an-email-campaign, SL
  = sms-listing-page-elements, SS = create-a-single-sms-message, SC =
  sms-create-a-campaign, AA = audience-analytics. `choose-a-target-segment`
  returned 404 at both /docs/ and /v1/docs/, so I covered the segment UI from
  AN, SS and AA instead. llms.txt lists .md versions of the pages and I used
  them. All screenshots are on cdn.document360.io/.../Images/Documentation/ and
  have empty alt text.

  **1. Layout / global shell**
  - **Left nav (dark, near-black, about #0F1221):**
    - Collapsed: a narrow icon rail [S, SL "image-1767621610170.png"]. The
  active item is a solid blue tile (about #1A2ECC) with a white icon.
    - Expanded: a wide panel with these sections, each with a caret: Favorites,
  Reports, Campaigns ›
  Messaging/Optimization/Recommendation/Experimentation/Architect/Search and
  Merchandising, Agent One, Audience, Insights, Behavior Analytics [S, EC
  "Screenshot at Sep 25 18-39-45.png"].
    - Flyout submenu items have a title, a one-line description and a star (to
  favorite).
  - **Top bar (white, thin):** icon buttons, a workspace dropdown, a user-email
  dropdown, and a dark "Get Support" button at the far right [S, SL].
  - **Page header:** home-icon breadcrumb ("⌂ > SMS"), then a large
  regular-weight H1 ("SMS" / "Email"). The primary button "+ CREATE" (uppercase)
  sits top-right [S, EC, SL].
  - **Below the header:** underline tabs. Email has Campaigns / Analytics /
  Templates; SMS has Campaigns / Prioritization [S].
  - **Page and content:** the page background is light grey (about #F1F2F5). The
  table sits in a white bordered container, full width [S]. Listings use a
  table, not cards [S].

  **2. Listing page anatomy**
  - **Filter row** [S, EC/SL]:
    - Multi-select dropdowns: "All Statuses" / "All Types" / "Select Tags".
  Selected values show as chips ("Running ×", "+1").
    - Search input with a magnifier.
    - "Filters" button (light-blue fill, SMS only).
    - Grey "Settings" button at far right.
    - The status dropdown has checkboxes plus an "Unselect All (2)" footer [S,
  EC].
  - **Columns:**
    - Email: STATUS | NAME ⇅ | TYPE | CREATED ON ⇅ | SEND DATE ⇅ | TAGS |
  ACTIONS | ⋮ [S, EC].
    - SMS: STATUS | NAME ⇅ (channel subtitle under the name, e.g. "SMS", "RCS
  Media") | TYPE | STARTS ⇅ | ENDS ⇅ | TAGS | ACTIONS | ⋮ [S, SL].
    - Headers are uppercase, letter-spaced, grey, on a light grey header row.
  - **Statuses:**
    - Email: Draft, Completed, Scheduled, Running, Awaiting Next Run, Paused
  [T+S, EC].
    - SMS adds Active and Test [T, SL].
    - Badge styles [S]:
      - Completed and Active: pale green fill (about #E8F6EC), green text (about
  #2E7D32).
      - Draft: grey fill (about #E6E7EA), grey text.
      - The others are not seen.
    - Badges are small rounded rectangles with sentence-case text.
  - **Types:** Single / Experiment / Recurring for email [T, EC]; Single / Cart
  Reminder / Welcome for SMS [S, SL].
  - **Tags:** outlined blue chips plus a "+2" overflow chip [S].
  - **Row actions:**
    - Blue line icons: link (SMS), analytics bar chart, info (i) for details
  [S].
    - Kebab ⋮ menu: Duplicate, Edit Tags, Pin/Unpin, Change Activation Status,
  Delete [T, EC/SL]. Pinned rows are highlighted yellow [T, EC].
    - Icons are greyed out when not applicable [S, SL].
  - **Pagination footer:** "Showing 1-3 of 3 items", centred « ‹ [1] › », and
  "10 items per page" input at right. The active page is a light-blue square [S,
  EC].
  - **Empty state:** not documented.

  **3. Creation flow**
  - **Create modal** [S, SS "image-1703749528748.png"]:
    - Title "Create Campaign" with × close, a Campaign Name input, and "Campaign
  Type" as stacked selectable rows (icon + bold title + description).
    - The selected row has a light-blue fill (about #C9DBFA) and a blue border.
    - Footer: text "Cancel" on the left, blue "Create" on the right.
  - **Steps:**
    - Email: Recipients › Design › Launch [S+T, AN].
    - SMS: Segments › Design › Goals › Launch [S, SS].
  - **Stepper:** horizontal, in a white top bar with breadcrumb-like text
  separated by chevrons.
    - Left of it, after a vertical divider, is a "← Email Campaigns" back link.
    - The current step is blue and semibold; the others are grey [S].
    - There is no numbered circle stepper.
  - **Body:** two columns. A white card holds the main form (about 2/3 width); a
  right rail (about 1/3) sits on the grey background [S].
  - **Sticky white footer:**
    - Left: "← Back to Campaigns" / "← Back to Design" / "← Back to Goals" as a
  text link.
    - Right: primary filled blue "Save and Continue". On Launch it becomes
  "Launch Campaign" or "Save as Draft" (the label follows the radio choice),
  next to an outlined "Test Message" [S].
  - **Launch step:**
    - Header "Ready to Go Live?" / "Ready to Run?" with a subtitle. A Campaign
  Name input sits top-right [S].
    - Radio group: Send Now / Send Later / Draft / Pause (Pause disabled). Send
  Later adds Start Date (calendar) and Start Time dropdown [S].
    - Collapsible sections with uppercase headers, caret ▼ and dotted dividers:
  Email Throttling, Send Time Optimized, Silent Hours, Campaign Tags, Frequency
  Capping, Notes [S].
    - Light-blue info banners (about #DCEEF8 fill, blue border, blue "!" icon)
  [S].
  - **Summary panel "Before You Launch"** (right rail) [S]:
    - Stacked white cards, each with a coloured top border, a shield/check icon
  in a tinted square, and coloured text.
    - Orange warning: missing fallback values, with "+ Click to assign".
    - Blue info: "Your current target audience is: 1,523,116 Recipients…".
    - Green success: "Your email is ready to go."
  - **Preview:** the inbox and email preview are on the right side in the Design
  step [T, AN]. I did not view it.

  **4. Audience / segment step**
  - **Email Recipients** [S, AN "image-1750759272015.png"]:
    - "SELECT RECIPIENT LISTS" and "EXCLUDE RECIPIENT LISTS" multi-select
  dropdowns with chips, plus helper text.
    - The right rail shows "Target Audience (?)" as a large solid-blue
  half-donut gauge, then the number "300" with the label "ESTIMATED AUDIENCE"
  (uppercase, small, letter-spaced).
    - A dashed divider, then an outlined blue "Get Exact Audience" button.
    - "All Contacts" is the default; "Save as New Segment" is available [T].
  - **SMS:** subscriber language selector, then segment groups from Predefined /
  Standard / Predictive / Saved. The right info area shows estimated recipients
  and readiness [T, SS].
  - **Segment drawer/modal** [S, AA "image-WBD1SXGA.png"]:
    - Title "Segments" with a description and ×, and underline tabs Tracked
  Segments / All Segments.
    - Inside, a bordered box with boxed tabs: Predefined / Standard / Predictive
  / Integrated / RFM / Saved.
    - A 2-column grid of segment tiles, each with a green line icon, a title
  (e.g. Cart Abandonment) and a description.
    - Footer: "Cancel" plus a disabled grey "Save and Track".
  - **Audience Analytics** [S, AA]:
    - KPI blocks: uppercase label + (?), a large number (12,168,530), then
  dotted-leader rows such as "Known Users ……… 10,833,216 (89.03%)".
    - "Reachability by Channel": one row per channel with a round grey icon,
  "Reachability on Email / 6,660,522 Users", a thin rounded grey progress bar
  with blue fill, and the % at right. Rows are separated by dotted lines.
    - Tracked-segment statuses: Processing / Active [T].

  **5. Visual language** (hex values are [I] estimates from screenshots)
  - **Colours:**
    - Primary: deep royal blue about #1A2ECC / #1631C9. It is used for buttons,
  active tabs, links and icons. The CREATE button has a slight blue-to-violet
  gradient (about #2233CC → #5B2BB5).
    - Text: dark slate about #1F2433; muted grey about #6B7280.
    - Background: #F1F2F5. Cards: white with about #E2E4E9 borders.
    - Success green: #2E7D32 on #E8F6EC. Warning orange: about #E07B00. Info:
  blue on #DCEEF8.
    - Purple about #5B2BB5 appears in charts [S, EC overview].
  - **Font:** a geometric sans, likely Gilroy or a similar face [I]. The name is
  not given in the text.
    - Weights and sizes: H1 about 28–32px regular; section labels small
  uppercase with wide tracking; body about 14px; bold is used sparingly
  (selected type titles).
  - **Shape and density:**
    - Radius is small: about 4px on inputs, buttons and badges; cards about
  2–4px.
    - Shadows are minimal, mostly flat with borders; the right-rail summary
  cards have a faint shadow.
    - Medium density with generous row heights (about 56–64px). Dotted dividers
  recur.
  - **Icons:** thin outline line icons, blue in actions and green in segment
  tiles [S].

  **6. Reusable components observed**
  - Navigation and layout: dark side nav with flyout, top utility bar,
  breadcrumb + H1 + primary CTA header, underline tabs, boxed tabs,
  text-breadcrumb stepper, sticky footer bar.
  - Lists and forms:
    - Data table with sortable headers (⇅), status badge, tag chip with "+N"
  overflow, icon actions + kebab, pagination with page-size input.
    - Multi-select dropdown with chips and checkboxes, search input, radio
  group, date picker, time dropdown, collapsible accordion sections.
  - Feedback and data:
    - Info banner (light blue), status/summary cards with a coloured top border.
    - Modal with footer (Cancel / primary), selectable option rows/tiles, side
  drawer (Segments).
    - Gauge (half donut), KPI number + dotted-leader breakdown, horizontal
  progress bars, (?) tooltips, success toast [T, AA].

  Gaps: no empty state is documented, there is no "Scheduled/Paused/Running"
  badge colour sample, the SMS Segments-step screenshot was not viewable (its
  filename contains `*`), and the design preview pane was not viewed.

› Message from general-purpose
  I wrote c:\insider_one_case\design\FEATURES.md. It lists **110 features
  (F01–F110)** and is the only file I created; nothing existing was changed. It
  is UTF-8 with LF line endings and written in Turkish.

  The document has five parts: an intro (screens, how navigation works, the
  global shell), feature tables per screen, the API endpoints, the data types
  shown on screen, and a `- [ ] Fxx` checklist.

  Navigation and storage, for your mapping:
  - **Page choice** comes from the hash: `#/dashboard` opens the Dashboard,
  every other hash opens Compose.
  - **`?c=<id>`** names the campaign. It is written with `replaceState` when a
  campaign is created and removed with `pushState` by "Yeni kampanya".
  - **Wizard steps** live only in component state; changing step does not touch
  the URL.
  - **No browser storage** is used (no localStorage, sessionStorage or
  IndexedDB).

  **Genel kabuk (App.vue)**
  - F01 Brand title
  - F02 "Yeni kampanya" tab (clears `?c=`, rebuilds ComposeView by key)
  - F03 "Dashboard" tab
  - F04 Hash-based page choice
  - F05 LLM mode badge (Mock modu / Claude (model))
  - F06 "API ulaşılamıyor" badge
  - F07 Defaults before /api/info answers (tr/en/de; editor hidden until limits
  arrive)
  - F08 Light/dark theme
  - F09 Reduced motion
  - F10 Mobile layout
  - F11 Browser tab title

  **Compose, wizard-wide**
  - F12 Open a campaign from `?c=` (no panel until loaded, opens at its current
  step)
  - F13 404 / load error
  - F14 StepBar
  - F15 Rule for which steps can be opened
  - F16 Slide animation between steps
  - F17 Geri / Vazgeç button
  - F18 Main bar button (actions and loading labels)
  - F19 Read-only after approval

  **Compose, Step 1 Brief**
  - F20 Brief textarea (max 2000)
  - F21 "Örnek brief" button
  - F22 Ctrl+Enter submits
  - F23 "Kitleyi çıkar" (POST /api/campaigns, at least 5 characters)

  - F25 Note that an approved brief cannot change
  - F26 Create error with "Ayrıntılar" (attempts)

  **Compose, Step 2 Kitle**

  - F30 Audience stat tiles

  - F36 "Tüm müşterilere gönder"

  **Compose, Step 3 Mesajlar**
  - F39 Live SSE generation
  - F40 Attempt badge (üretiliyor / düzeltiliyor n/3)
  - F41 Retry notes
  - F42 Fallback template notice
  - F43 Stream error
  - F44 Language tabs with status dots
  - F45 Cross-language mismatch warning

  - F51 Server validation issues (jumps to the language with the first problem)

  - F54 SMS phone preview

  - F57 "Yeniden üret"

  - F61 Approved badge

  **Compose, Step 4 Onay**
  - F62 Prerequisite empty state
  - F63 Hemen / Zamanla toggle
  - F64 DateTimeField picker
  - F65 Quick time shortcuts
  - F66 Default send time
  - F67 Time-limit hint (30 days / offer's last day)
  - F68 Schedule validation messages
  - F69 Approval summary
  - F70 Estimated-numbers note
  - F71 Warnings list
  - F72 Two-step approval (POST approve, optional scheduled_at)
  - F73 Approve error

  **Compose, Step 5 Gönderim**
  - F75 Status badge

  - F77 Cancel scheduled send (modal, POST cancel)
  - F78 Cancelled notice

  - F87 Not-yet-approved empty state
  - F88 "Dashboard'a git"

  **Dashboard**
  - F89 Heading and "Yeni kampanya"
  - F90 List polling every 2 s (GET /api/campaigns?limit=100)
  - F91 Stale-list error
  - F92 Four stat tiles
  - F93 Send-rate calculation (last 10 s)
  - F94 Loading
  - F95 Empty state
  - F96 Campaign table and opening a row via `?c=`
  - F97 Title (64 characters) and audience sub-line
  - F98 Row status badge

  **Shared UI**
  - F106 Modal accessibility (Esc, backdrop, focus trap, scroll lock)
  - F107 Button loading spinner
  - F108 Animated numbers
  - F109 Progress bar accessibility
  - F110 General API error format

  **Endpoints (section 3):**
  - GET /api/info
  - POST /api/campaigns
  - GET /api/campaigns/{id}
  - PUT /api/campaigns/{id}/content
  - POST /api/campaigns/{id}/messages/stream (SSE events: attempt, delta, retry,
  validated, fallback, done, error)
  - POST /api/campaigns/{id}/approve
  - POST /api/campaigns/{id}/cancel
  - GET /api/campaigns?limit=100
  - GET /api/system

  **One existing quirk (recorded under F102):** the countdown in the dashboard
  table's Zaman column is measured from the moment the table was first shown and
  never updates afterwards. The list polling does not refresh it either. The
  countdown on the wizard's Gönderim step does update, every 15 s.

  Karar vermeniz gerekenler (DESIGN.md §5)

  1. Yazı tipi: Geist mi kalsın (önerim, yeni paket gerekmez) yoksa Inter mı
     eklensin?
  2. Koyu tema: Insider panelinde koyu tema yok, ama bizde var. Koyu tema yeni
     renklerle korunsun mu?
  3. Yarım halka grafikleri: Kitle ve başarısızlık sayıları yeni bir çizimle
     gösteriliyor. Kabul mü, yoksa düz çubuk mu kalsın?
  4. Hemen / Zamanla: İki parçalı anahtar yerine seçenek satırları olsun mu?
  5. Dashboard'daki donan geri sayım: Görünüm işinde aynen kalacak. Ayrı bir iş
     olarak düzeltilsin mi?
  6. Adlandırma: "Dashboard" yerine "Kampanyalar" denilsin mi?
  7. /preview sayfası: Dönüşüm bitince silinsin mi?

  cevaplarınızı da ekleyebilirsiniz.
> Henüz onaylamıyorum. Önce /preview'da şu düzeltmeleri yap, sonra tekrar dur.

  Düzeltmeler

  1. Boyut: 1920px'de içerik çok dar ve küçük. Ana alan max-width: 1440px
  olsun,
     temel font 15px olsun. Input, textarea ve label'lar orantılı büyüsün.
  2. Hizalama: Akış barı, içerik kartları ve alt buton barı aynı genişlikte ve
  aynı
     kenarda olsun. Brief ekranındaki alan stepper'dan dar kalmasın. Mesajlar
  ekranında
     sol %60, sağ önizleme %40 olsun; önizleme sticky dursun.
  3. Önizleme paneli: Eski hâline dönsün (ekteki görsel): SMS için telefon
  mockup'ı,
     email için "Gelen kutusu" penceresi. Eski bileşeni git geçmişinden bul ve
  yeniden kullan,
     sadece token'ları uyarla. TR/EN/DE sekmesi değişince önizleme de o dile
  geçsin.

  1920px ve 1366px'de ekran görüntüsü alıp kontrol et.

  Soruların cevapları

  1. Yazı tipi: Geist kalsın.
  2. Koyu tema: Korunsun, yeni renklerle uyarla.
  3. Yarım halka: Başarısızlık nedenleri için kabul. Kitle sayıları için düz
  kartlar/çubuk yeterli.
  4. Hemen / Zamanla: Seçenek satırları olsun.
  5. Donan geri sayım: Bu işte dokunma, ayrı bir iş olarak not al, sonra
  düzeltiriz.
  6. Adlandırma: "Kampanyalar" olsun.
  7. /preview: Dönüşüm bitince silinsin.

  designSystem.test.ts içindeki dört kontrolü onaydan sonra yeni tasarıma göre
  güncelleyebilirsin.

  Düzeltmeler

  1. Boyut:
     - Ana alan artık en fazla 1440px, temel yazı 15px.
     - Ölçeğin geri kalanı da büyüdü: küçük yazı 12,5/14, büyük 17/21, sayfa
       başlığı 32px, büyük harfli etiketler 12px.
     - Buton ve tek satırlı alanlar 40px yüksekliğinde. Metin kutularının iç
       boşluğu ve satır aralığı orantılı arttı.
  2. Hizalama:
     - Akış barı, içerik kartları ve alt buton barı aynı sütunda, aynı
       kenarlarda duruyor.
     - Brief kartı artık tam genişlikte.
     - Mesajlar ekranı %60 düzenleyici, %40 önizleme olarak bölündü. Önizleme
       yapışık duruyor.
     - 1366×768'de önizleme ekrandan uzun kalıyordu. Yüksekliğini ekrana
       sınırladım, sığmayan kısım kendi içinde kayıyor.
     - 960px altında tek sütuna geçiyor.
  3. Önizleme paneli:
     - Telefon mockup'ı ve "Gelen kutusu" penceresi geri geldi.
     - Orijinal dosyalara dokunmamak için iki bileşeni parts/PhonePreview.vue
       (frontend/src/views/preview/parts/PhonePreview.vue) ve
       parts/EmailPreview.vue
       (frontend/src/views/preview/parts/EmailPreview.vue) olarak kopyaladım.
       Yapıları ve mantıkları aynı, fillSample da yine utils/preview.ts'ten
       geliyor; yalnızca sabit renkler token oldu.
     - Koyu temada iki mockup gerçek bir ekran gibi açık renkte kalıyor.
     - TR/EN/DE sekmesi değişince önizleme de o dile geçiyor; Deutsch'ta telefon
       ve e-posta Almanca görünüyor.

  Cevaplarınız işlendi

  - Kitle: Sağ paneldeki yarım halka kalktı. Yerinde büyük kişi sayısı, renkli
    bir çubuk ve dört satır var (iki kanaldan, yalnız SMS, yalnız e-posta,
    ulaşılamayan). Yarım halka yalnızca başarısızlık nedenlerinde kaldı.
  - Diğer kararlar: Hepsi DESIGN.md (design/DESIGN.md) §5'e yazıldı.
  - Donan geri sayım: §7'ye ayrı iş olarak not edildi ve sonraki oturumlar için
    hafızaya kaydedildi.

  Önizlemeyi açmak

  make dev, sonra http://localhost:8080/preview.

  Dev ortamı zaten açıksa imajın bir kez yeniden kurulması gerekiyor; make dev
  bunu --build ile yapıyor.

  Belirli bir ekranı doğrudan açmak için:
  - /preview?screen=flow&step=messages&state=ready&lang=de
  - /preview?screen=flow&step=audience&state=ok

> Henüz onaylamıyorum. /preview'da şu düzeltmeleri yap, sonra tekrar dur.

  1. Alt buton barı

  "Geri" ve "Onayla" butonlarının etrafındaki beyaz kutuyu kaldır. Butonlar
  doğrudan sayfa
  arka planında dursun: "Geri" içerik alanının sol kenarına, "Onayla" sağ
  kenarına hizalı olsun.
  Tüm adımlarda (Brief, Kitle, Mesajlar, Onay, Gönderim) aynı şekilde olsun.

  2. Ekran hâlâ küçük

  - İçerik 1920px'de hâlâ dar. max-width: 1600px yap, yanlardaki padding 40px
  olsun.
  - Temel font 16px olsun. Başlıklar, input'lar, sekmeler ve label'lar da
  orantılı büyüsün.

  3. SMS ve email önizlemeleri daha dar olsun

  - Telefon mockup'ı en fazla ~300px genişlikte olsun ve ortalansın.
  - "Gelen kutusu" penceresi en fazla ~420px genişlikte olsun ve ortalansın.
  - Önizleme sütunu ~%35, düzenleme alanı ~%65 olsun.

  4. Responsive

  - ≥1280px: iki sütun (düzenleme + sticky önizleme).
  - 768–1279px: tek sütun, önizleme düzenleme alanının altına geçsin.
  - <768px: sol menü üstte hamburger menüye dönüşsün; akış barı yatay
  kaydırılabilir olsun
    ya da sadece "3/5 · Mesajlar" göstersin; butonlar tam genişlikte ve alt
  alta olsun.
  - Bütün ekranlara uygula: Kampanyalar listesi (mobilde tablo kart listesine
  dönsün),
    Brief, Kitle, Mesajlar, Onay, Gönderim dashboard'u.

  Kontrol

  1920, 1366, 1024, 768 ve 390px genişlikte ekran görüntüsü al. Taşma, yatay
  kaydırma ya da
  üst üste binen eleman olmadığını kontrol et. onaylarsam projeye uyarla.

● I made all four fixes in /preview and checked every screen at 1920, 1366,
  1024, 768 and 390px. No page overflows or scrolls sideways at any width, and
  in the screenshots I reviewed nothing overlaps. Type checking passes and the
  existing 263 tests pass. No existing file was changed, nothing is committed,
  and I've stopped again.

  Yapılanlar

  1. Alt buton barı: Beyaz kutu kalktı, butonlar doğrudan sayfa zemininde
     duruyor. "Geri" içerik kolonunun sol kenarında, ana buton sağ kenarında;
     beş adımda da aynı.
  2. Boyut:
     - Kolon en fazla 1600px, yan boşluk 40px.
     - Temel yazı 16px. Ölçeğin geri kalanı da büyüdü: sayfa başlığı 34px,
       etiketler 13px, buton ve tek satırlı alanlar 44px yüksekliğinde.
  3. Önizlemeler:
     - Düzenleme %65, önizleme %35.
     - Telefon en fazla 300px, "Gelen kutusu" en fazla 420px; ikisi de ortalı.
     - Önizleme sütunu yapışık duruyor.
  4. Responsive:
     - 1280px ve üstü: İki sütun, önizleme yapışık.
     - 768–1279px: Tek sütun, önizleme ve sağ panel içeriğin altına geçiyor.
     - 768px altı:
       - Sol menü yerine üst barda hamburger menü var.
       - Akış barında yalnızca "← Kampanyalar · 3/5 · Mesajlar" görünüyor.
       - Butonlar tam genişlikte ve alt alta, ana buton üstte.
       - KPI'lar tek sütun, etiket ve değer satırları alt alta.

  İstediğinizden farklı yaptığım iki şey

  - Kart listesi 1024px'in altında başlıyor: İstediğiniz sınır 768px'ti, ama
    768px'te tablo kendi kartının içinde yana kayıyordu ve dil çipleri ikiye
    bölünüyordu.
  - Dar ekranda breadcrumb kısalıyor: Kelimenin ortasından kesildiği için
    telefonda yalnızca "ev › bulunulan sayfa" gösteriliyor, sığmazsa "…" ile
    bitiyor.

  Ölçüm notu

  - 390px: Headless Edge 500px'ten dar pencere açmıyor ve kaydırma çubuğu da yer
    kaplıyor. Bu yüzden her sayfayı tam o genişlikte bir iframe içinde açtım ve
    taşmaları ?check=1 ile sayfanın kendi içinde saydım.
  - Bulunan tek taşma: 390px'te Gönderim adımında "Onaylandı" etiketi 2px
    taşıyordu. Akış barını telefonda iki satıra izin verecek şekilde düzelttim.

  Yeni önizleme parametreleri

  - tags=0: Sarı Fxx etiketlerini gizler.
  - menu=1: Hamburger menüyü açık gösterir.
  - check=1: Taşma kontrolünü çalıştırır.

  Kırılım tablosu ve kontrol sonucu DESIGN.md (design/DESIGN.md) §8'de.
  Onaylarsanız ("ONAYLIYORUM") aynı yapıyı projenin kendi dosyalarına
  uygulayacağım.

> ONAYLIYORUM. Uygulamaya geç, aşağıdaki ek isteklerle birlikte.

  1. Özellik kaybı olmayacak

  FEATURES.md'deki 110 özelliğin (F01–F110) TAMAMI yeni arayüzde çalışacak. Her
  ekranı
  bitirdiğinde ilgili maddeleri işaretle. Sonunda işaretlenmemiş tek madde
  kalmasın,
  bana listeyi göster.

  2. Gereksiz açıklama metinlerini kaldır

  Arayüzde kullanıcıya bir şey anlatan gereksiz yardımcı cümleler olmasın.
  Örnekler:
  - "Kaynak: model. Kaydedilmemiş düzenleme var; "Onayla" kaydeder."
  - "İzin vermeyen ya da telefonu / e-postası olmayan kişilere mesaj
  gönderilmez."
  - "Önizleme: kişi adı yerine örnek olarak "Elif" kullanılır."
    Tüm ekranları bu gözle tara, benzer cümleleri kaldır. Ama şunları koru:
  - Gerçek veri taşıyan bilgiler, örneğin "SMS'e ulaşılabilir: 1.870 / izinsiz:
  312" gibi sayılar
  - Hata ve uyarı mesajları (limit aşımı, geçersiz LLM çıktısı vb.)
  - Kaydedilmemiş değişiklik varsa küçük bir nokta ya da rozet yeterli, cümle
  olmasın.
    Kaldırdığın metinlerin listesini sonunda bana ver.

  3. Zamanlanmış gönderim: en fazla 1 ay sonrası takvımden secılsın

  - Tarih seçicide geçmiş tarihler ve bugünden itibaren 30 günden sonraki
  tarihler seçilemesin.
  - Backend'de de aynı kural olsun: geçmiş ya da 30 günden ileri bir tarih
  gelirse 422 dönsün.
    Bunun için backend'e dokunmana izin veriyorum. Bu kural için backend ve
  frontend testi ekle.
  - Zamanlanmış kampanya listede "Zamanlandı · 24 Kas 14:00" gibi görünsün,
  iptal edilebilsin.

  4. Önizlemeyi kapat

  Dönüşüm bitince /preview route'unu, preview.html dosyasını ve önizlemeye özel
  kodu
  (sarı şerit, Fxx etiketleri, ?check=1 vb.) tamamen kaldır.

● Now the sending step: DeliveryProgress gets a KPI group per channel, an
  overall progress bar, and a half-ring failure chart with a legend. I'm
  building the half-ring component first, carried over from the preview.
  · summary

● Bash(cd /c/insider_one_case/frontend/src; python - <<'EOF'

       1 +<script setup lang="ts">                                       
       2 +// A half ring: each part is an arc of the semicircle in propor
         +tion to its value                                              
       3 +// (used for the reasons deliveries failed). The slot sits in t
         +he middle, under the arc.                                      
       4 +import { computed } from 'vue'                                 
       5 +                                                               
       6 +export interface Arc {                                         
       7 +  value: number                                                
       8 +  color: string

  2. Kaldırılan açıklama metinleri

  Tam tablo DESIGN.md §9'da.
  - Brief:
    - "Onaylanan kampanyanın brief'i değiştirilemez." Alan salt okunur ve akış
      barında "Onaylandı" rozeti var.
  - Kitle:
    - "İzin vermeyen ya da telefonu / e-postası olmayan kişilere mesaj
      gönderilmez." Ulaşılamayan sayısı sağ panelde.
    - Sıfır eşleşme uyarısındaki "Brief'i değiştirmeyi deneyin."
  - Kitle sorusu:
    - "İzin vermemiş olanlara yine de gönderilmez."
    - "Ya da brief'i kime göndereceğinizi yazarak güncelleyip tekrar deneyin."
  - Mesajlar:
    - "Kaynak: model. Kaydedilmemiş düzenleme var; “Onayla” kaydeder." Yerine
      "Model metni / Hazır şablon / Elle düzenlendi" rozeti, "Kaydedilmedi"
      rozeti ve sekmede nokta.
    - "Önizleme: kişi adı yerine örnek olarak “Elif” kullanılır."
    - "(karakter sınırlarına sabit pay olarak sayılır)". Açıklama artık değişken
      çiplerinin ipucunda.
    - "Önceki deneme sınırlara uymadığı için model düzeltiyor:"
    - Şablon uyarısındaki "lütfen metni düzenleyin. Şablon da tüm sınırlara
      uyar."
    - Dil uyuşmazlığındaki "İndirim oranı, tutar, tarih ve {{değişkenler}} her
      dilde aynı olmalı".
  - Onay:
    - "En fazla 30 gün sonrası" / "En geç … (teklifin son günü)". Sınırı artık
      seçicinin kendisi uyguluyor.
    - "Kitle gönderim anında yeniden belirlenir; kişi sayısı değişebilir."
      Yerine "Tahmini sayılar" rozeti.
    - "Onaya geçmek için önce mesajları üretip kaydedin." Yerine "Mesajlar henüz
      kaydedilmedi."
  - Gönderim:
    - "– bu sayfa kendiliğinden güncellenir"
    - "Kitle gönderim anında belirlenir; kimsenin burada olması gerekmez."
  - Kampanyalar:
    - "Sayfa her 2 saniyede güncellenir."
    - KPI'ların altındaki dört açıklama satırı. Artık (?) simgesinin
      ipucundalar.
    - "Bir brief yazarak ilk kampanyayı oluşturun."

  Hata ve uyarı mesajları, "Emin misiniz?" onayı, iptal penceresi, kitle kararı
  notu ve durum bildirimleri kaldı.
